Set in contemporary Iran. A Separation is a compelling drama about the 
dissolution of a marriage. Simin wants to leave Iran with her husband Nader 
and daughter Termeh. Simin sues for divorce when Nader refuses to leave 
behind his Alzheimer-suffering father. Her request having failed, Simin returns to 
her parents' home, but Termeh decides to stay with Nader. 
When Nader hires a young woman to assist with his father in his wife's absence, 
he hopes that his life will return to a normal state. However, when he discovers 
that the new maid has been lying to him, he realizes that there is more on the 
line than just his marriage. 
Runtime is 123 minutes

Happy Holidays! 
Next year will mark a decade of D - and what a wild ride it's been. From our many spellbinding 
dialogues with Steve Jobs, to visits from political heavyweights like McCain and Gore, to "hoodiegate" 
with Mark Zuckerberg, the D conferences have had an undeniable and profound impact on the digital 
revolution. 
Just like the D conference of years past, DI0 will provide a provocative and unscripted perspective 
into today's digital landscape from technology's most powerful luminaries. As always, our unyielding 
brand of journalism will take center stage, fueling the explosive culture of innovation that has come 
to define the digital era. 
Earlier this month DI0 SOLD OUT to previous D, Dive and AsiaD attendees. This sell out came in 
record time. To show our appreciation for your long standing interest in D, we would like to offer you 
the opportunity to add yourself to the waitlist, prior to opening it up to the general public. We do 
anticipate some attrition and encourage you to add yourself to the waitlist as soon as possible.

htto://allthingsd.com/conferences/d/d10/register/d10newreg

Rancho Palos Verdes on May 29-31, 2012 and we will begin announcing names soon. For more 
background on D10 and the full portfolio of D conferences visit 
hnp://allthingsd.cornicetegory/conferencesi.
Please don't hesitate to contact the DI0 registration office toll-free at 866-416-DWSJ (3975), or via 
email at registration@AIIThingsD.com with any questions. 
We look forward to celebrating a decade of D with you in May 2012! 
Best Regards, 
Walt & Kara
